class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res=[]
        cur=[]
        def backtrack(i):
            if i=len(s) and len(cur)==4:
                res.append(".".join(cur))
                return
            if len(cur)>4 or i>len(s):
                return
            if s[i]=='0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i+1)
                cur.pop()
                return
            j=0
            while j<4 and i+j<len(s):
                if int(s[i:i+j+1])<256:
                    cur.append(s[i:i+j+1])
                    backtrack(i+j+1)
                    cur.pop()
                j+=1
        backtrack(0)
        return res

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res = []
        cur = []

        def backtrack(i: int) -> None:
            if i == len(s):
                if len(cur) == 4:
                    res.append(".".join(cur))
                return

            if len(cur) >= 4:
                return

            if s[i] == '0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i + 1)
                cur.pop()
                return

            for j in range(1, 4):
                if i + j <= len(s):
                    part = s[i:i+j]
                    if int(part) <= 255:
                        cur.append(part)
                        backtrack(i + j)
                        cur.pop()

        backtrack(0)
        return res

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res = []
        cur = []

        def backtrack(i: int) -> None:
            if i == len(s):
                if len(cur) == 4:
                    res.append(".".join(cur))
                return

            if len(cur) >= 4:
                return

            if s[i] == '0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i + 1)
                cur.pop()
                return

            for j in range(1, 4):
                if i + j <= len(s):
                    part = s[i:i + j]
                    if int(part) <= 255:
                        cur.append(part)
                        backtrack(i + j)
                        cur.pop()

        backtrack(0)
        return res

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res = []
        cur = []

        def backtrack(i: int) -> None:
            if i == len(s):
                if len(cur) == 4:
                    res.append(".".join(cur))
                return

            if len(cur) >= 4:
                return

            if s[i] == '0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i + 1)
                cur.pop()
                return

            for j in range(1, 4):
                if i + j <= len(s):
                    part = s[i:i + j]
                    if int(part) <= 255:
                        cur.append(part)
                        backtrack(i + j)
                        cur.pop()

        backtrack(0)
        return res
```

The final answer should be exactly:

```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res = []
        cur = []

        def backtrack(i: int) -> None:
            if i == len(s):
                if len(cur) == 4:
                    res.append(".".join(cur))
                return

            if len(cur) >= 4:
                return

            if s[i] == '0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i + 1)
                cur.pop()
                return

            for j in range(1, 4):
                if i + j <= len(s):
                    part = s[i:i + j]
                    if int(part) <= 255:
                        cur.append(part)
                        backtrack(i + j)
                        cur.pop()

        backtrack(0)
        return res
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res = []
        cur = []

        def backtrack(i: int) -> None:
            if i == len(s):
                if len(cur) == 4:
                    res.append(".".join(cur))
                return

            if len(cur) >= 4:
                return

            if s[i] == '0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i + 1)
                cur.pop()
                return

            for j in range(1, 4):
                if i + j <= len(s):
                    part = s[i:i + j]
                    if int(part) <= 255:
                        cur.append(part)
                        backtrack(i + j)
                        cur.pop()

        backtrack(0)
        return res
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def restoreIpAddresses(self, s: str) -> List[str]:
        res = []
        cur = []

        def backtrack(i: int) -> None:
            if i == len(s):
                if len(cur) == 4:
                    res.append(".".join(cur))
                return

            if len(cur) >= 4:
                return

            if s[i] == '0':
                cur.append(s[i])
                backtrack(i + 1)
                cur.pop()
                return

            for j in range(1, 4):
                if i + j <= len(s):
                    part = s[i:i + j]
                    if int(part) <= 255:
                        cur.append(part)
                        backtrack(i + j)
                        cur.pop()

        backtrack(0)
        return res
